Verification

First wave: original MSES data rebuilds C.air exactly; XFOIL matches below the drag rise

Scope: Uncommitted tools/airfoil_gen tree on top of the current worktree commit

Four tasks are done and were re-checked here. Drela's table builder, run on his original MSES polars, reproduces all three shipped tables byte for byte. On the original sections, XFOIL matches MSES drag within about 2 counts up to M 0.6. Above that it misses the wave drag, so the drag rise needs a model.

The pipeline that now exists

The shipped tables came from MSES polars run through Drela's POLFIT program. That path is now reproduced locally, and XFOIL is built to replace the MSES step.

  • Geometry: the original MSES section files, C and E series, t/c 0.09 to 0.145, from Drela's public TASOPT 2.16 distribution.
  • Reference data: 178 MSES polar files, parsed into one CSV of 5177 rows.
  • Table writers: POLFIT and a Julia reader and writer that round-trip the file format without changes.
  • Still to build: the XFOIL batch driver and the wave-drag model.

Nothing staged, tool tree present, four tasks done

[HIGH] TASK-14 - Rebuild C.air from the original MSES polars with POLFIT (pipeline baseline) (ac: 2/2)

Record the scope: commit, nothing staged, the new tool tree and task statusr1

Rebuilding the shipped tables exactly

Running POLFIT on the original polars, capped at Mach 0.81, reproduces C.air, Cl.air and E.air byte for byte.

  • This identifies the column meanings: the table's cdp is the MSES pressure drag, wave drag included. The table's cdf is total drag minus pressure drag.
  • The polar headers fix the run settings: Re 2×1072\times10^72×107, Ncrit 9, transition forced at 3 % chord on top and 5 % on the bottom.
  • The Julia round-trip test passes 18 of 18 checks for each table. These include equal TASOPT airtable structs and equal airfun outputs at 1000 random points.

C.air: byte-identical to shipped

xtrf =   0.030 (top)        0.050 (bottom)

airfile round trip: C |   18     18

XFOIL against MSES on the same section

On the 12 % section with the MSES settings, XFOIL tracks MSES closely at low Mach. It separates once MSES develops wave drag.

MachclXFOIL cdMSES cdMSES wave drag
0.30.60.007060.007080
0.30.90.007850.007810
0.60.60.007280.00740
0.60.90.008200.009780.00152
  • Below the drag rise, XFOIL drag agrees with MSES to within about 2 counts. One count is 10−410^{-4}10−4.
  • The pitching moment is the weak spot. At M 0.6, XFOIL's cm is about 0.01 to 0.02 less negative than MSES on the 9-13 % sections.
  • The stored subagent table shows the same pattern for all seven sections.

Independent XFOIL runs on c120 and matching MSES rows

MSES M=0.6 cl=0.9 CD=0.00978 CDp=0.0048 CDw=0.00152 CM=-0.1042

| c120 | 0.6 | 1.894 | 1.390 | 72.5 | 74.0 | -1.5 | -1.4 | +19.1 |

Read the subagent's stored XFOIL-vs-MSES table and the C.air drag-divergence tabler8

Why a wave-drag model is still needed

XFOIL rises only gently with Mach because its compressibility correction has no shocks. MSES drag climbs steeply near the drag-divergence Mach number.

  • The drag-divergence Mach number of C.air at t/c 0.12 and cl 0.6 is about 0.745. At t/c 0.09 and cl 0.4 it is about 0.78.
  • At cl 0.9, M 0.78, XFOIL returned an unphysical drag of order 1. This is a non-converged point that the batch driver must detect and reject.
  • The wave-drag model will be calibrated against the MSES wave-drag column, which is now available point by point.

XFOIL Mach sweep on c120, including the bad point

0.9 0.78 1.00292

Sweep XFOIL in Mach on c120 and plot it against the stored MSES drag-rise polarsr6

Stored drag-divergence table

0.0900,0.400,0.7795,0.7650

Read the subagent's stored XFOIL-vs-MSES table and the C.air drag-divergence tabler8

XFOIL build

The build script rebuilds XFOIL in about 13 seconds and runs a NACA 0012 case without graphics.

  • NACA 0012 at Re 2×1072\times10^72×107, M 0.3 and four degrees gives cl 0.4846 and cd 0.00572. This matches the subagent's smoke test.
  • XFOIL prints a floating-point exception note on exit. The results are not affected.

Rebuild and headless point

CL =  0.4846

Rebuild XFOIL from the script and run one headless NACA 0012 pointr2

import csv, numpy as np, matplotlib; matplotlib.use("Agg"); import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
rows=[r for r in csv.DictReader(open("tools/airfoil_gen/reference/mses/mses_polars.csv")) if r["family"]=="c" and abs(float(r["tc"])-0.12)<1e-6]
xf=np.loadtxt("/tmp/wk/xf.txt", ndmin=2)
fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(8,3.8))
for cl,col in ((0.6,"C0"),(0.9,"C3")):
    m=[(float(r["Mach"]),float(r["CD"])*1e4,float(r["CDw"])*1e4) for r in rows if abs(float(r["cl_target"])-cl)<1e-6]; m.sort()
    m=np.array(m); ax.plot(m[:,0],m[:,1],"-",color=col,label=f"MSES polar (stored), cl {cl}")
    ax.plot(m[:,0],m[:,2],":",color=col,lw=1,label=f"MSES wave drag, cl {cl}")
    x=xf[np.isclose(xf[:,0],cl)]; ax.plot(x[:,1],x[:,2]*1e4,"o",color=col,mfc="none",label=f"XFOIL (this run), cl {cl}")
ax.set_xlabel("Mach number"); ax.set_ylabel("section drag cd (counts)"); ax.set_ylim(0,250); ax.set_xlim(0,0.84)
ax.set_title("c120 section, Re 2e7, Ncrit 9, transition 0.03/0.05"); ax.grid(alpha=.3); ax.legend(fontsize=7,ncol=2)
fig.tight_layout(); fig.savefig(".walkthrough/figs/dragrise.png",dpi=130)
EOF
